# V17 paired voice comparison — part 3/3

These accepted V17 supporting registers serve the existing Humor/Voice ledger responsibilities as volume evidence appendices. Publication and whole-tranche adjudication remain separate. Original discovery, assignments and authored-time statements remain exact; current errata are explicitly external. Same-model conditional independence and receiving limits are recorded in the [V17 receiving review](../09%20Audits%20and%20Manifests/KONOSUBA_V17_RECEIVING_REVIEW.md). Exact source members are text/part0009.html (C1), part0010.html (C2), part0011.html (C3), part0012.html (F), part0013.html (Ep); excluded bodies/images/holdouts remain unopened in this continuation.

Fixed original-order records 163–243, 81 records. Order is P99 then I144, not selected by result. [Navigation/scope/exact-text catalog](KONOSUBA_V17_VOICE_COMPARISON_REGISTER.md) governs all V references. Each table value is compact JSON with one reversible HTML escape for &,<,>,|; JSON carries literal newline/backslash/quote escapes. {"@exact_text":"ID"} expands to the exact string in the named catalog. This is lossless factoring of repeated text, not a shortened rationale. Every unique initial rationale is printed in full, in its own labeled row; its value is factored out of the otherwise exact initial field bundle and restored by the inverse proof. Proof parses actual Markdown rows, reverses escapes/references, and compares every projected field to the immutable initial inputs. Private full novel bases/windows, ruby XML/pairs and long selected quotations are omitted by the declared projection; their hashes/locators and initial analytical metadata remain. No global spacing/reflow is applied to original strings. Native literal joins verify text and location, not by themselves speaker, addressee, literal-gloss accuracy or pragmatic judgment; those remain substantive receiving responsibilities.

### IV17-066

| id | "IV17-066" |

| producer_initial_disposition_exact | "SUPPORTED_LOCAL" |
| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "Aqua’s serious plea37 and visibly anxious state38 precede Kazuma’s41 question/offer to make return possible while continuing enjoyable life, with51’s remembered-wish framing. He shifts from public self-defense20 to ordinary relational explanation and a practical offer to her. Care already exists from C2, so this is newly explicit/intensified particular care rather than first adoption. Recalled48/50 are historical content, not newly received current dialogue or a calm-state prerequisite." |
| independent_initial_role_phase_exact | "ACTIVE_PARTICULAR_CARE" |
| independent_initial_speech_adequacy_exact | "ADEQUATE" |
| independent_initial_function_direction_exact | "SUPPORTED_ACTIVE_MIXED" |

| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "Eris teases Kazuma’s response to Aqua and reports his smiling face292 while continuing an established advisory/helper role. The observable call and reaction ground the interaction, not a newly inferred mature personality from gentleness. Her own exceptional-rule declaration243 belongs to a different task moment. This auxiliary change in topic and teasing function supports no main-four J01 test or acoustic comparison." |
| independent_initial_role_phase_exact | "AUXILIARY" |
| independent_initial_speech_adequacy_exact | "ADEQUATE" |
| independent_initial_function_direction_exact | "DESCRIPTIVE_CONTINUITY" |
| independent_full_initial_rationale_exact | "Eris moves between rule explanation, helper action and familiar teasing about Kazuma’s visible smile. | producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "Known companions still fighting and newly usable weakening316 independently motivate tactical search. Kazuma’s326 police proposal is a concrete resource/alternative-provider request to Eris, followed by further feasibility questions after the mass-harm warning327. It fits a broad task-specific request function while outsourcing danger and entertaining destructive options. Care is already active, the proposal is hypothetical rather than a completed handoff, and before309 already concerns the mission; no universal altruistic voice is claimed." |

This changes assessed opponent risk within already existing mission urgency. The comparison is auxiliary, with Kazuma alone as listener; no party audience or Aqua-status mapping transfers. Claims and the visible card are source inputs, not a new universally accurate judgment of Kazuma’s abilities." |

This is an auxiliary authority negotiation, not Aqua’s status branch or a presumption of fear from sound." |

| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "The King’s actual intention18/19 to return threatens the pre-established separation plan and allies, independently intensifying tactical care. Kazuma’s after26/30 class disclosure and delaying negotiations are tactical, but lack a specific protective command/resource request/handoff; they chiefly taunt and manage the enemy’s attention. The absent companions need not be direct listeners for the branch to apply, so no companion-only exception rescues it. Sparse comparable baseline13–23 and an already active plan make this a bounded adverse function rather than retirement." |
| independent_initial_role_phase_exact | "ACTIVE_MISSION_CARE" |
| independent_initial_speech_adequacy_exact | "ADEQUATE" |
| independent_initial_function_direction_exact | "MIXED_TACTICAL_PROVOCATION" |
| independent_full_initial_rationale_exact | "Kazuma’s absent-friend protection was established before the duel; the after uses class taunts and the adventurer card to buy engagement/time. These can be tactical provocations but are not plainly a protective command, resource request or handoff. No fresh caring event is isolated, and an enemy addressee is not itself disqualifying." |

Prior60 boasts automatic evasion; keep the genuine object-value objection as adjacent rather than forcing a relational mapping." |

| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "The existing gift and Kazuma’s736 care warning support renewed gratitude; a gift need not be newly received to matter. The relational request is present but comparison/circularity is limited; prior734 already offers a crystal, so no abrupt first caring voice is claimed." |

| independent_initial_speech_adequacy_exact | "LIMITED" |
| independent_initial_function_direction_exact | "MIXED_RECIPROCITY_AND_JAB" |
| independent_full_initial_rationale_exact | "The old gift is long known, but actual new mana handoff735 and Kazuma’s gentle cost warning736 matter now; no newly received-gift floor is imposed. Megumin737 explicitly repays Kazuma and then jabs Darkness.
